function safeString(value, maxLength = 500) { if (value === null || value === undefined) return ""; if (!["string", "number", "boolean"].includes(typeof value)) return ""; return String(value).replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F]/g, "").trim().slice(0, maxLength); } function corsHeaders(request, env) { const origin = request.headers.get("Origin") || ""; const configured = String(env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN || "").trim().replace(/\/$/, ""); const requestOrigin = new URL(request.url).origin; const headers = { "Vary": "Origin", "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, POST, PATCH, OPTIONS", "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, Authorization", "Access-Control-Max-Age": "86400" }; if (origin && (configured ? origin === configured : origin === requestOrigin)) headers["Access-Control-Allow-Origin"] = origin; if (total > maxBytes) { try { await reader.cancel(); } catch (_) {} return { error: "too_large" }; } chunks.push(part.value); } const bytes = new Uint8Array(total); let offset = 0; for (const chunk of chunks) { bytes.set(chunk, offset); offset += chunk.byteLength; } try { return { value: JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) }; } catch (_) { return { error: "invalid" }; } } function makeReference() { const d = new Date(); const date = `${d.getUTCFullYear()}${String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`; return `MPX-REQ-${date}-${crypto.randomUUID().replaceAll("-", "").slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`; } async function notifyOwner(env, requestData) { if (!env.RESEND_API_KEY || !env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || !env.NOTIFY_EMAIL) return false; } const consentFields = { business: ["requestConsent"], artist: ["artistContactConsent"], consultation: ["consultationConsent"], newsletter: ["newsletterConsent"], suggestion: ["suggestionConsent"], "partner-proposal": ["termsAcknowledged", "dataConsent"], shipment: ["shipmentConsent"], "payment-notice": ["paymentNoticeConsent"] }; for (const key of (consentFields[kind] || [])) { if (fields[key] !== "yes") return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Please confirm the required consent before submitting." }, 400); } const emailForValidation = fields.email || fields.requestEmail || fields.artistEmail || fields.consultationEmail || fields.partnerEmail || fields.senderEmail || fields.suggestionEmail || ""; if (emailForValidation && !/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(emailForValidation)) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Enter a valid email address." }, 400); const email = safeString(fields.email || fields.requestEmail || fields.artistEmail || fields.consultationEmail || fields.partnerEmail || fields.senderEmail || fields.suggestionEmail, 254).toLowerCase(); const now = new Date().toISOString(); // Privacy-preserving per-IP throttle: store only a SHA-256 hash, never the raw IP. const requestIp = request.headers.get("CF-Connecting-IP") || "unknown"; const requestIpHash = await sha256Hex(requestIp); const requestWindow = new Date(Date.now() - 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(); const requestAttempts = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM request_attempts WHERE ip_hash = ? AND created_at > ?").bind(requestIpHash, requestWindow).first(); if (Number(requestAttempts?.total || 0) >= 20) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Too many submissions from this connection. Please wait 10 minutes and try again." }, 429); const attemptSaved = await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO request_attempts (ip_hash, created_at) VALUES (?, ?)").bind(requestIpHash, now).run(); if (!attemptSaved.success) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Could not apply the request safety check. Please try again later." }, 500); // Basic duplicate throttle: same email and form type may submit once per 45 seconds. if (email) { const recent = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM requests WHERE email = ? AND kind = ? AND created_at > ? LIMIT 1") .bind(email, kind, new Date(Date.now() - 45000).toISOString()).first(); if (recent) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "A similar request was just received. Please wait a little before submitting again." }, 429); } const reference = makeReference(); const result = await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO requests (reference, kind, email, fields_json, status, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'new', ?)") .bind(reference, kind, email || null, JSON.stringify(fields), now).run(); if (!result.success) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "We could not save your request. Your entries have not been cleared; please try again later." }, 500); const notifySent = await notifyOwner(env, { reference, kind, fields, createdAt: now }); return json(request, env, { success: true, reference, stored: true, notificationSent: notifySent, message: "Request saved." }, 201); } if (url.pathname === "/api/admin/requests" && request.method === "GET") { if (!env.ADMIN_TOKEN || request.headers.get("Authorization") !== `Bearer ${env.ADMIN_TOKEN}`) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Unauthorized. Check the administrator token." }, 401); if (!env.DB) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "D1 database is not configured." }, 503); const status = url.searchParams.get("status") || "all"; let result; if (status === "all") result = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, reference, kind, email, fields_json, status, created_at FROM requests ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200").all(); else if (ALLOWED_STATUSES.has(status)) result = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, reference, kind, email, fields_json, status, created_at FROM requests WHERE status = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200").bind(status).all(); else return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Invalid status filter." }, 400); const requests = (result.results || []).map(r => ({ id: r.id, reference: r.reference, kind: r.kind, email: r.email, fields: JSON.parse(r.fields_json || "{}"), status: r.status, createdAt: r.created_at })); return json(request, env, { success: true, requests }); } const statusMatch = url.pathname.match(/^\/api\/admin\/requests\/(\d+)\/status$/); if (statusMatch && request.method === "PATCH") { if (!env.ADMIN_TOKEN || request.headers.get("Authorization") !== `Bearer ${env.ADMIN_TOKEN}`) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Unauthorized." }, 401); const parsedBody = await readJsonLimited(request, 1000); if (parsedBody.error) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: parsedBody.error === "too_large" ? "Request is too large." : "Invalid JSON." }, parsedBody.error === "too_large" ? 413 : 400); const body = parsedBody.value; const status = safeString(body.status, 30); if (!ALLOWED_STATUSES.has(status)) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Invalid request status." }, 400); const result = await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE requests SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(status, new Date().toISOString(), Number(statusMatch[1])).run(); if (!result.success || result.meta?.changes === 0) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Request not found or status unchanged." }, 404); return json(request, env, { success: true, status }); } // Serve website assets from this same Worker origin after API routes. if (env.ASSETS && (request.method === "GET" || request.method === "HEAD")) { return env.ASSETS.fetch(request); } return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Not found." }, 404); } }; /* D1 MIGRATION â€” run once in Cloudflare D1 SQL console; do not drop existing tables: -- Run this migration once in the existing Cloudflare D1 database used by Marrakechpixim. -- Do not drop or recreate the existing requests table.
